# Skills · D · part 147

847 entries, differential-evolution-proposal-tuning to dig. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **differential-evolution-proposal-tuning**: Use when fitting Bayesian models on real parameter spaces where an appropriate scale and orientation for the jumping distribution is hard to choose, e.g. nonlinear mixed-effects models, and multi-chain MCMC should adapt jumps automatically via gamma = 2.38/sqrt(2d). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-astronomy/tree/HEAD/collections/astronomy/v1/leaves/differential-evolution-proposal-tuning))
- **differential-evolution-solver**: Differential evolution solver for global optimization using population-based mutation and crossover strategies. ([SpectreDeath/Em-Cubed](https://github.com/SpectreDeath/Em-Cubed/tree/HEAD/skills/OPTIMIZATION/differential-evolution-solver))
- **differential-exon-usage-analysis**: Use when long-read (PacBio/ONT) FASTQs have short-read cell-type assignments and you need per-exon PSI quantification and HIPP-vs-PFC delta-PSI tests, or single-cell-vs-spatial ΔΨ concordance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-exon-usage-analysis))
- **differential-expression**: Bulk transcriptomics differential expression with count-aware modeling, design validation, contrast handling, thresholded exports, and publication-ready DE figures. ([Runchuan-BU/BioClaw](https://github.com/Runchuan-BU/BioClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/differential-expression) · ★411 · 5 copies)
- **differential-expression**: Find differentially expressed marker genes between groups using scanpy rank_genes_groups with Wilcoxon test. The comparison target is flexible — between cell types, clusters, spatial domains, slices, ROIs, or any user-defined grouping. ([chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent](https://github.com/chenyhvvvv/STAT-agent/tree/HEAD/src/stat_agent/skills/differential-expression) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **differential-expression**: 差异表达分析：做统计建模、表达倍数收缩、多重校正与结果可视化。当需要比较不同条件间的基因表达时使用。 ([qing1989620/xinxin-local](https://github.com/qing1989620/xinxin-local/tree/HEAD/backend/resources/skillnet/seed/skills/differential-expression) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **differential-expression-analysis**: Use when analyzing bulk RNA-seq or microarray expression data to identify differentially expressed genes between two biological groups (case vs control), with volcano plots and heatmap visualization. NOT for:single-cell RNA-seq, methylation analysis, non-expression data. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome-med-research-skills/Data%20Analysis/differential-expression-analysis) · ★2.1k · 5 copies)
- **differential-expression-analysis**: Use when a tncRNA (or other small RNA) count matrix with biological replicates for control and stress-treated samples (abiotic or biotic) needs significance testing to catalog stress-responsive tncRNAs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-analysis))
- **differential-expression-analysis**: Use when raw or imputed scRNA-seq count matrices (genes as rows, cells as columns, e.g. GSE75748 ESC/DEC) need DE gene lists between subpopulations, or when imputation impact on DE accuracy must be benchmarked against a bulk RNA-seq reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-analysis))
- **differential-expression-analysis-algorithm-selection**: Use when after preprocessing and normalizing count matrices from transcriptomics or other omics data, when you need to identify differentially expressed features between experimental groups and must choose between edgeR (for flexible formula/contrast designs), DESeq2 (for designs with explicit. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-analysis-algorithm-selection))
- **differential-expression-analysis-bulk-and-single-cell**: Use when comparing single-cell vs bulk differential expression agreement for a two-population scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. H1 vs DEC), or when benchmarking denoising methods (DCA, MAGIC, SAVER, scImpute) by bulk-LFC correspondence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-analysis-bulk-and-single-cell))
- **differential-expression-analysis-endothelial-cells**: Use when benchmarking single-cell batch-integration methods on multi-condition mixtures and you need a quantitative readout of whether across-condition biological differences (e.g. tissue-specific endothelial DE genes) survive integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-analysis-endothelial-cells))
- **differential-expression-analysis-limma**: Use when a reference-based cell classifier needs marker genes that are specific to a reference cell type not only against sibling reference types but also against cell types present in an external atlas (e.g. MCA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-analysis-limma))
- **differential-expression-analysis-of-tumor-infiltrating-lymphocytes**: Use when comparing bulk or single-cell RNA-seq of CD8 TILs between immunotherapy (anti-PD-1) and control conditions, or when scoring an input scRNA-seq dataset for CD8 TIL transcriptomic states to test EM-like signature enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-analysis-of-tumor-infiltrating-lymphocytes))
- **differential-expression-analysis-with-deseq2**: Use when comparing two conditions (e.g., BNLx vs SHR strains) at transcript level after RSEM quantitation, especially to test whether a modified transcriptome changes the DE call set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-analysis-with-deseq2))
- **differential-expression-aucpr-assessment**: Use when evaluating an imputation or preprocessing method's effect on differential expression accuracy in scRNA-seq data where a ground-truth set of DE genes is known, especially as a function of the zero-count (dropout) proportion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-aucpr-assessment))
- **differential-expression-auroc-assessment**: Use when benchmarking a feature selection algorithm (e.g., DUBStepR vs. HVGDisp, HVGVST, trendVar, devianceFS, HLG, M3Drop/DANB, GiniClust) on scRNA-seq data and you need a DE-detection AUROC against independently ascertained cell type annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-auroc-assessment))
- **differential-expression-based-node-scoring**: Use when you have case/control RNA-seq count matrices (e.g. TCGA ESCA tumor vs normal) and need to weight nodes of a CLIP-derived RBP-ncRNA network before running fRNC's global or local module search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-based-node-scoring))
- **differential-expression-batch-correction**: Remove batch effects from RNA-seq data using ComBat, ComBat-Seq, limma removeBatchEffect, and SVA for unknown batch variables. Use when correcting batch effects in expression data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/differential-expression/batch-correction) · ★1)
- **differential-expression-candidate-regulator-screening**: Use when you have a scRNA-Seq matrix with a multipotent cell population and competing downstream lineages (e.g. SHARE-Seq hair-follicle TACs vs IRS/HS, or Ranzoni fetal-liver MPPs vs GP/MEMP), and you need to shortlist overexpressed regulatory factors to feed into DICE priming analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-candidate-regulator-screening))
- **differential-expression-cluster-validation**: Use when RCC (or a consensus clustering run over candidate k values) has produced clusters and you must decide whether each cluster is a genuine subtype before subdividing further, using differential gene expression as the quality gate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-cluster-validation))
- **differential-expression-cutoff-classification**: Use when a task requires thresholding differential-expression results into up/down/neutral classes and annotating per-panel counts on volcano/scatterplot facets from DE tables, matrices+metadata, or MultiAssayExperiment objects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-cutoff-classification))
- **differential-expression-deseq2**: Use when fitting a bulk RNA-seq differential-expression model with DESeq2 from raw integer counts or a tximport object. Requires a defensible design, explicit reference levels and contrasts, replication, model diagnostics, and a shrinkage method compatible with the requested coefficient. ([Linxira-OS/linxira-skills](https://github.com/Linxira-OS/linxira-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-expression-deseq2))
- **differential-expression-detection-evaluation**: Use when a normalization method for single-cell RNA-seq must be evaluated for its effect on DE detection, using simulated two-group scenarios with known DE genes and true fold changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-detection-evaluation))
- **differential-expression-empirical-bayes-moderation**: Use when after fitting a linear model with lmFit on voom-transformed or log2-normalized RNA-seq or microarray expression matrices, apply eBayes moderation to moderate gene-wise variance estimates before extracting top differentially expressed genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/differential-expression-empirical-bayes-moderation))
- **differential-expression-feature-extraction**: Use when a simulated bulk multi-omics dataset (e.g. MOSim RNA-seq + miRNA-seq + TF) must yield a defined DEG/regulator feature set with ground-truth regulatory signs (activator vs repressor) for downstream validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-feature-extraction))
- **differential-expression-fold-change-calculation**: Use when you have a SAM significance output (samr-based Significance Analysis of Microarrays) plus a quantile-normalized expression matrix and need heatmaps of top-ranked gene lists that cluster samples by response class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-fold-change-calculation))
- **differential-expression-fold-change-ranking**: Use when you have differential expression results (gene-level log fold-changes) from perturbed 3T3-L1 adipocyte microarray data and need a fold-change-ranked gene list as input to clusterProfiler GSEA for GO term enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-fold-change-ranking))
- **differential-expression-gene-identification**: Use when you have scRNA-seq count matrices with known rare-cell labels (e.g., artificial splatter data with group.prob = c(0.99, 0.01) or the packaged 1%Jurkat.h5) and need a validated set of DE genes and non-DE background genes for robustness/sensitivity tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-gene-identification))
- **differential-expression-gene-list-extraction**: Use when a CCIVR Table.csv/Summary.csv of cis-NAT pairs carries RNA-seq expression columns (TPM, fold_change, padj) and the goal is to split partner genes into up/down lists for GO enrichment (e.g., DAVID GOTERM_BP_FAT). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-gene-list-extraction))
- **differential-expression-gene-list-processing**: Use when RNA-seq gene expression data must be converted into a differentially expressed gene set and tested for significant overlap with differential histone modification calls (e.g. H3K27me3 or H3K9me3). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-gene-list-processing))
- **differential-expression-gene-overlap-statistics**: Use when you have cluster-derived key genes from scQA (e.g. labels.txt chained from a prior clustering task) and need to confirm they agree with published marker genes, hub genes, or DEGs for the same cell type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-gene-overlap-statistics))
- **differential-expression-gene-scoring**: Use when validating single-cell drug-response predictions on a drug-treated scRNA-seq dataset by scoring DEGs and correlating scores with Monocle3-style pseudotime or sensitive/resistant probability labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-gene-scoring))
- **differential-expression-go-enrichment**: Use when a Leiden (or equivalent) clustering over an integrated embedding — e.g. SSGATE's 30-dim joint transcriptome-proteome output — needs functional interpretation via per-cluster marker genes and GO term annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-go-enrichment))
- **differential-expression-gold-standard-annotation**: Use when benchmarking DE detection methods on simulated scRNA-Seq data where ground-truth differential genes must be extracted from known true counts (e.g. SymSim population pairs 1v2, 1v4, 2v4) before any observed-count analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-gold-standard-annotation))
- **differential-expression-group-planting**: Use when you need a controlled synthetic scRNA-seq dataset with known ground-truth cell groups defined by distinct DE gene combinations, e.g. to benchmark cluster-merging or cluster-significance tools against planted truth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-group-planting))
- **differential-expression-log-transform-visualization**: Use when signed differential-expression scores for a PCa-vs-normal RNA-seq contrast must be rendered as a Cytoscape color gradient (−1200 to 600) over expanded KEGG pathway nodes to show up/down regulation per gene or aggregated node. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-log-transform-visualization))
- **differential-expression-log2foldchange-scoring**: Use when raw sequencing counts from paired control/condition anchored genomic regions must be converted to a continuous biological activity score for a score-based motif enrichment analysis such as MEPP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-log2foldchange-scoring))
- **differential-expression-logfc-extraction**: Use when a completed multi-method DEG results table (e.g. ExpHunterSuite output with vote-threshold calls and log2FC columns) needs a specific gene's logFC record extracted for reporting, validation, or downstream GSEA-style reuse. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-logfc-extraction))
- **differential-expression-method-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating which multi-sample trajectory differential expression method controls false discoveries on single-cell data, especially by counting called genes on a null dataset where no gene is truly differential. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-method-benchmarking))
- **differential-expression-method-comparison**: Use when evaluating or selecting a cluster-free differential expression method for multi-sample, multi-condition single-cell data where a simulated cohort with known ground-truth DE labels is available for validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-method-comparison))
- **differential-expression-modeling**: Use when you need per-gene DEG testing on RNA-seq count data with an unpaired design (no within-subject pairing, no kinship/random effects), using kimma's kmFit run_lm=TRUE on TMM-normalized log2 CPM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-modeling))
- **differential-expression-module-filtering**: Use when you already have a cell-lineage-specific GRN plus driver regulators with influence scores and need gene modules tied to those regulators, then per-cell activity scores via AUCell. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-module-filtering))
- **differential-expression-overlap-scoring**: Use when benchmarking scRNA-seq simulation methods on their ability to regenerate ground-truth differential expression between cell groups, or when reproducing the paper's DEG functionality scores (e.g., Lun = 0.90) from simmethods/simpipe outputs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-overlap-scoring))
- **differential-expression-plotting**: Use when an experimental biologist has a gene expression table (e.g. ma_example.csv) from differential expression analysis and needs an MA plot rendered via a web interface instead of R code. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-expression-plotting))
- **differential-expression-profile-labelling**: Use when building a synthetic bulk RNA-seq time-course with ground-truth DEG classes — e.g. seeding a simulator from a real sample's NB mean/dispersion and needing labelled induction/repression/flat temporal profiles. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-profile-labelling))
- **differential-expression-pvalue-seeding**: Use when you have case/control RNA-seq data (e.g. GSE172114 COVID-19, 46 critical vs 23 non-critical cases), have computed per-gene DE P-values, and need to extract a perturbed subnetwork from a reference interactome such as the KEGG graph (5007 nodes / 44755 edges). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-pvalue-seeding))
- **differential-expression-pydeseq2**: Run DESeq2-style bulk RNA-seq differential expression in Python with PyDESeq2, including count matrix validation, design formula checks, size-factor estimation, dispersion fitting, Wald/LRT contrasts, shrinkage guidance, and exportable result tables. Use when the project is Python-first or needs DESeq2-compatible methods without switching to R. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/differential-expression/pydeseq2) · ★1)
- **differential-expression-quadrant-classification**: Use when a CSV of pre-computed log2 fold-changes and adjusted p-values for two Treatment-vs-Control comparisons must be screened to identify extreme or moderate expression variations by fold-change thresholds and significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-quadrant-classification))
- **differential-expression-ranking**: Use when you have completed a two-group or multi-group differential expression analysis with computed logFC values (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-ranking))
- **differential-expression-recovery-f1-scoring**: Use when a normalization method must be quantitatively judged on simulated scRNA-seq data where the ground-truth normalized values and planted DE/cluster labels are known, and RMSE/F1 tables (3 settings x 5 scenarios) are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-recovery-f1-scoring))
- **differential-expression-result-interpretation**: Use when you have fitted a linear model to gene expression data (microarray, RNA-seq, qPCR, or proteomics) across multiple samples and need to compute gene-level test statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/differential-expression-result-interpretation))
- **differential-expression-results**: Use when filtering, annotating, exporting, visualizing, and interpreting bulk RNA-seq differential-expression results. Preserves complete tested results, explains NA values correctly, separates statistical from biological thresholds, and prevents post-hoc or clinical overclaiming. ([Linxira-OS/linxira-skills](https://github.com/Linxira-OS/linxira-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-expression-results))
- **differential-expression-results-filtering**: Use when a DESeq2 results table (with gene_id, log2FoldChange, padj, baseMean) contains NA rows or unfiltered fold changes and must be cleaned before annotating siRNA seed matches or running deseq_fc_ecdf comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-results-filtering))
- **differential-expression-signature-querying**: Use when you have a short DE gene signature (e.g. 27–71 genes from GEO/MSigDB C7 studies of disease, smoking, or toxic-chemical exposure) and want to identify statistically enriched environmental gene modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-signature-querying))
- **differential-expression-significance-thresholding**: Use when you have a precomputed DE table, expression/count matrix with metadata, or a MultiAssayExperiment object and need faceted volcano plots (log2 fold-change vs -log10(p-value)) with thresholded significance classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/differential-expression-significance-thresholding))
- **differential-expression-similarity-based-cell-filtration**: Use when preprocessing Perturb-seq/CROP-seq data (e.g., crop_stimulated expression_profile.txt + perturb_information.txt) to purge cells carrying invalid sgRNA edits before topic-model building in MUSIC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-similarity-based-cell-filtration))
- **differential-expression-summary-statistics-generation**: Use when you have a two-group scRNA-seq comparison (e.g. DEC vs EC, CD4+ vs CD8+ T cells) and need per-gene beta/se summary statistics as input to iDEA or other GSE methods that consume DE summary statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-summary-statistics-generation))
- **differential-expression-summary-table-generation**: Use when a main folder hosts one sub-directory per pairwise comparison (each with pre-computed scorecard outputs) and you need per-gene totals across all comparisons to separate common gene-expression changes from condition-specific ones. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-summary-table-generation))
- **differential-expression-t-test-analysis**: Use when you have a simulated two-group scRNA-Seq dataset with known truly differentially expressed gene labels and need to quantify how imputation (e.g., AutoClass) restores DE signal lost to dropout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-t-test-analysis))
- **differential-expression-table-querying**: Use when a gene of interest (e.g. 'insulin') must be matched to the cell types in which it is specifically expressed, using precalculated per-cluster differential expression/marker scores already ingested into Cellenium's database across one or more scRNA-seq studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-table-querying))
- **differential-expression-testing**: Use when DC3 subpopulation-specific FPKM profiles are available and transcription factors must be tested for differential expression between subpopulations to build a key-regulator candidate list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-testing))
- **differential-expression-testing-after-doublet-removal**: Use when a doublet-contaminated scRNA-seq dataset with known or expected DEG labels is available and you need to quantify whether doublet removal (by Chord, DoubletFinder, scds, etc.) improves differential expression results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-testing-after-doublet-removal))
- **differential-expression-testing-single-cell**: Use when you have a single-cell TAR or gene expression matrix with cell-cluster labels and need to identify features (uTARs or genes) that are differentially expressed per cluster, e.g. to direct annotation efforts to biologically significant transcripts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-testing-single-cell))
- **differential-expression-threshold-estimation**: Use when overlap counts between differential expression genes and differential accessibility peaks (or SNP/gene statistics) are available and an objective, bootstrap-justified logFC or P-value cutoff is needed instead of an arbitrary threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-threshold-estimation))
- **differential-expression-threshold-filtering**: Use when building cancer cell-intrinsic subtype marker gene sets for pre-clinical model subtyping, i.e. when DE results from tumor cohorts must be stripped of stromal/immune signals absent from cell lines, organoids and PDXs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-threshold-filtering))
- **differential-expression-thresholding**: Use when raw RNA-seq count data from infected and control conditions (e.g. GEO GSE147507 / GSE148729 lung samples) must be reduced to binary expressed/not-expressed gene lists under |log2FC|&gt;1, padj&lt;0.05 thresholds to drive rFASTCORMICS model pruning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-thresholding))
- **differential-expression-timeseries-de**: Analyze time-series RNA-seq data using limma voom with splines, maSigPro, and ImpulseDE2. Identify genes with dynamic expression patterns. Use when analyzing time-series or longitudinal expression data. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/differential-expression/timeseries-de) · ★1)
- **differential-expression-trajectory-visualization**: Use when a longitudinal cohort has per-patient timepoints relative to a clinical event (respiratory nadir) and you need to determine how early differential expression of candidate genes emerges and when levels return to baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-trajectory-visualization))
- **differential-expression-utar-filtering**: Use when a single-cell TAR expression analysis has produced differentially expressed uTARs (e.g., from Wilcoxon rank-sum testing across clusters) that need functional or gene-level annotations via sequence homology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-expression-utar-filtering))
- **differential-facet-verifier**: Audit, compare, and verify multi-provider evidence dossiers using explicit facet availability semantics, absence claimability gates, and 3-valued differential outcomes. Do not use for generic line text diffing or single-provider analysis without differential evidence. ([SpectreDeath/Brain-Harness](https://github.com/SpectreDeath/Brain-Harness/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/differential-facet-verifier))
- **differential-family-analysis**: Finds, evaluates, clusters, validates, and extends XOR, modular, truncated, higher-order, and mixed-domain differential properties. Use when: The target has nonuniform propagation of differences through nonlinear operations, slow diffusion, controllable input structures, useful truncated patterns, or a published differential that may transfer. ([regenscheid/crypto-analysis-skills-general](https://github.com/regenscheid/crypto-analysis-skills-general/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-family-analysis) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **differential-fate-selection-testing**: Use when a single common trajectory (e.g., slingshot sds) spans multiple conditions and the goal is to test whether condition-level cell abundance differs between lineages (differential fate selection), after topologyTest has not rejected a common topology. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-fate-selection-testing))
- **differential-feature-cross-referencing**: Use when you have a list of theoretical biotransformation candidate m/z values and a separate table of dysregulated/DE features from untargeted LC-HRMS data, and need to preannotate which candidates map to observed features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-feature-cross-referencing))
- **differential-feature-refactor**: Use when a feature already exists in code but has drifted from — or was never reconciled with — its intended design, and someone must decide what changes before a refactor is scoped. Triggers: two parallel implementations of the same thing, code that reads as dead but may be a ratified extension point, an architecture nobody can tell from accumulated cruft, or a refactor about to be scoped off review documents instead of decisions. ([Arasz/ai-raccoon](https://github.com/Arasz/ai-raccoon/tree/HEAD/.ai-badger/skills/differential-feature-refactor) · ★6 · 8 copies)
- **differential-fuzzer**: Information about the differential fuzzer tool, how to run it and use it catch bugs in Turso. Always load this skill when running this tool ([tursodatabase/turso](https://github.com/tursodatabase/turso/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/differential-fuzzer) · ★25k · 1.2k installs · 8 copies)
- **differential-gene-analysis-rna-seq**: Performs differential gene expression analysis on RNA-seq count matrices using DESeq2 and generates volcano, PCA, heatmap, and significant-gene outputs. ([luokai0/ai-agent-skills-by-luo-kai](https://github.com/luokai0/ai-agent-skills-by-luo-kai/tree/HEAD/ai-agent-skills/22-clawhub-skills/differential-gene-analysis-rna-seq) · ★14)
- **differential-gene-analysis-rna-seq**: if (!require("BiocManager", quietly = TRUE)) { install.packages("BiocManager", repos = "https://cloud.r-project.org/") } ([Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge](https://github.com/Lord1Egypt/awesome-skill-forge/tree/HEAD/community/clawhub/d/differential-gene-analysis-rna-seq) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when RNA-seq differential expression results must be integrated with ChIP-seq peak-to-promoter annotations to identify loci where two regulators both bind and co-regulate the same genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--897ffb58b3b719ce))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when cluster assignments from an unsupervised expression clustering solution (e.g., SRIQ, 6 clusters at diameter 0.63) exist and per-cluster up-/down-regulated gene lists and GO terms are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--bb0dbef597552b6c))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when raw RNA-seq FASTQ files and sample metadata need end-to-end processing (QC, STAR/BBMap alignment, featureCounts quantification, RUVSeq normalization) followed by DEG testing, or when a two-factor CIPHER expInfo schema must be extended to multi-condition or time-series contrasts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--a7f0782b3afeada2))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have paired copy-number/epi-DNA alteration calls (e.g. GISTIC2.0 focal SCNAs) and matched RSEM log2 expression for the same tumor samples, and need cis (in-peak) and trans (genome-wide) DE genes per alteration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--ae832575a983d053))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a joint ST-SM AnnData with integrated Leiden cluster labels and need cluster-specific marker genes and metabolites (e.g., Imm_4 markers CYP2J2, ACSM2A, Phosphatidylglycerol, PA (24:0/24:1(15Z))). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--f3ff3499e639c41f))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when a gene expression matrix (e.g., RNA-Seq counts or microarray intensities) has two metadata-defined sample groups and the goal is to flag genes significantly up- or down-regulated between them, as in tumor vs non-tumor LIHC comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--056e77fc6fcd8a83))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a raw bulk RNA-seq count matrix (csv/txt) plus optional sample metadata and need per-gene differential expression results (log2FoldChange, Benjamini-Hochberg adjusted p-values) without scripting R/DESeq2 directly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when Salmon-quantified transcript counts from simulated RNA-seq reads (Polyester-generated FASTQ with a known injected DE pattern) must be analyzed with DESeq2 and GO enrichment validated for teaching workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when a gene-by-cell Smart-seq2 (or comparable) scRNA-seq count matrix is paired with per-cell mtDNA clone/subclone labels and the goal is to test if two subclones are transcriptionally distinct and which Hallmark pathways separate them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--f2e66968a0998a57))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a gene-level count table from an RNA-seq experiment (real, spike-in, or simulated) and need robust DEG calls whose method-specific assumptions are unknown or likely to matter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--40640222da726826))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when evaluating a batch-correction method on scRNA-seq data (e.g. two healthy samples from the same condition) and you need DEG counts, survival-relevant DEG signatures, or a comparison of over-correction vs. batch-noise removal across methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--1400fe9af37327bc))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when cells have already been assigned to clusters by SINCERA cluster.assignment and you need per-cluster differentially expressed genes to characterize cell types or build gene signatures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--bd9a0102d9548e71))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when a Seurat scRNA-seq object with annotated clusters/subclusters (e.g. PBMC3k) needs cluster-specific marker genes, and genes expressed in any non-target cluster must be excluded from the marker set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--492b551a814c232d))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq case/control data must be reduced to per-cell-cluster DEG profiles before connectivity-based drug repurposing (ASGARD GetDrug/DrugScore), or when benchmarking which scRNA-seq DE method maximizes downstream AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--8ba44679cb5b9dad))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when validating predicted perturbation-response transcriptomes (e.g., scButterfly-B output on PT_PBMC) by comparing per-cell-type DEG profiles to ground truth with top-100 DEG overlap and R2 metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--b409822bfce98862))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a scRNA-seq count matrix (.h5ad) with cell-type and condition/disease annotations and need per-cell-type DEG calls between conditions (e.g. COVID-19 vs normal, infarct vs control), with ranked gene lists and volcano plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--71f43caeb342febf))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when a normalized gene/ortholog expression matrix with sample metadata is available and the goal is a ranked table of DEGs at a defined FDR threshold (e.g., FDR &lt; 0.05 with |log2FC| &gt; 1.5, or FDR &lt; 0.1 for salamander-style analyses). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--bab10576d8e50f0e))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when comparing gene expression between two groups of RNA-seq samples (e.g., high vs low GSVA-score strata or tumor vs normal) and you need DEGs filtered by p-value, FDR and logFC thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--f5f86c100b71f196))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when a raw read count matrix plus sample metadata (e.g. GSE147507 severity study) must be tested for infected-vs-mock differential expression under |log2FC|&gt;1 and adj p&lt;0.05 thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--c6f9dc37474fab50))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when comparing gene-level expression between exactly two user-defined cohorts from a parsed PDAC expression dataset and you need per-gene logFC and padj values plus a volcano plot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--14c078497c220df8))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when raw scRNA-seq expression matrices for two or more FACS-sorted or annotated cell subsets are available and signature genes distinguishing terminal cell types are needed for fate prediction or marker discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--b57b895c27da66ca))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when rare cells detected from a large scRNA-seq dataset (e.g. 4238 FiRE-detected PBMC cells) have been clustered and specific clusters must be biologically characterized and assigned to known reference sub-types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--e1cc7eb534f07a80))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a Seurat object with graph-based clusters (e.g. severe vs healthy COVID-19 bronchoalveolar/peripheral cells) and need DE gene lists plus their enriched environmental/gene modules with padj and fold enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--9b59c988edff0070))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have GEMLI-predicted symmetric/asymmetric cell fate lineages with cell type annotations and need to identify DEGs between cells of the same type that followed different fate decisions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--db454c82a03e266f))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when when you have RNA-seq count matrices (from HTSeq, featureCounts, Salmon, kallisto, or RSEM quantification) and need to test for differential expression between two or more treatment groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/differential-gene-expression-analysis))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when building a malignant-vs-normal epithelial classifier feature set from multiple annotated scRNA-seq training datasets, or when you need to combine per-dataset DEG lists into one pan-cancer gene panel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--b12c33e11a77b9bb))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a single-cell gene expression matrix plus cluster labels (e.g., SSRE-inferred cell type labels) and need to detect marker genes per cluster, expecting recovery of known markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--86fba29601f53791))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when scRNAseq clusters (e.g., from LCMV1/LCMV2 CD44+ T cell 10X data) already exist and you need genes defining each cluster versus all others, plus subtype label assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis--4b72b649a5f43cdb))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis-of-novel-cluster**: Use when a Cell BLAST query leaves query cells unassigned (rejected under the default hit cutoff), indicating a putative novel cell type that must be validated by differential expression of marker genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis-of-novel-cluster))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis-of-t-cell-subsets**: Use when you have clustered an mvTCR joint embedding of paired scRNA-seq/TCR data into antigen-specific and bystander T cell clusters and need to characterize their transcriptional differences, e.g. effector CD8+ T cells from SARS-CoV-2 PBMCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis-of-t-cell-subsets))
- **differential-gene-expression-analysis-riboseq**: Use when ribosome profiling FastQ data from at least two conditions (reference vs comparison) must be processed end-to-end to identify genes with significant differential translational regulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-analysis-riboseq))
- **differential-gene-expression-marker-extraction**: Use when a reference scRNA-seq dataset with known cluster labels must yield per-cluster marker gene sets to score or assign target cells, e.g. before scid_multiclass mapping or a top-N-marker thresholding baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-marker-extraction))
- **differential-gene-expression-query-processing**: Use when you have a DE gene list from a disease-vs-control contrast (e.g., GEO microarray study) and need to identify statistically enriched environmental pathway gene modules via Fisher exact test with Benjamini-Hochberg FDR correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-query-processing))
- **differential-gene-expression-stratification**: Use when RNA-seq expression changes across an environmental perturbation (e.g., 0 min vs 60 min cadmium in yeast, GSE153609) must be binned into ranked gene groups to subset downstream chromatin analyses such as +1 nucleosome dyad composites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-stratification))
- **differential-gene-expression-testing**: Use when a merged, quantile-normalized (preprocessCore 'normalize.quantiles') expression matrix of staged cells must be reduced to a gene set that captures developmental variation before trajectory/pseudotime analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-testing--0abbb8eef4b08cfa))
- **differential-gene-expression-testing**: Use when pseudo-bulked gene-level count matrices from 3'-tag scRNA-seq (scUTRquant output) are available and you need to identify genes whose mRNA abundance differs between two cell types or conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-testing--96515886b4d08abe))
- **differential-gene-expression-testing**: Use when simulated or real single-cell data contain a binary covariate (e.g. stimulant/mutational status) and you need to quantify how well DE-based tests recover genes with covariate-pseudotime interactions versus a dedicated interaction model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-testing--d3356bcc6be1bfb7))
- **differential-gene-expression-ttest**: Use when you have Spark-normalized scRNA-seq expression (tall/wide format) plus K-means cluster assignments, and need per-gene two-sample t-test DE rankings between every pair of clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-expression-ttest))
- **differential-gene-gene-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have normalized sequencing count data plus sample metadata with a categorical subgroup variable (e.g. TCGA-BRCA SUBTYPE = BRCA_Her2 vs BRCA_LumA) and need group-specific gene-gene interaction networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-gene-correlation-analysis))
- **differential-gene-module-activity-testing**: Use when single-cell gene module activities (GMVs) have been inferred per cell in a VEGA latent space and you need statistically testable, per-module activity differences between conditions (e.g., Trametinib vs. DMSO). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-module-activity-testing))
- **differential-gene-regulation-testing**: Use when you have manifold-aligned network coordinates for genes from two single-cell conditions (scTenifoldNet output) and need to call significantly differentially regulated genes with p-values and FDR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-regulation-testing))
- **differential-gene-regulatory-network-inference**: Use when you have two gene expression matrices (tab-separated, rows = genes, columns = samples, first column named 'Gene') from two conditions and want a context-specific differential network beyond plain differential expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-gene-regulatory-network-inference))
- **differential-geometer**: Think and work like an expert Differential Geometer. Use when a task calls for Differential Geometer judgment. Reasons from connections, curvature, and holonomy; fixes Lee vs Besse/MTW Riemann signs; uses SageManifolds/xAct/Cadabra, Chern–Weil and Atiyah–Singer index theory, and model-space checks (S^n, flat tori) while treating chart artifacts, torsion misuse, and CAS convention drift as first-class failure modes. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/differential-geometer/skills/differential-geometer) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **differential-geometer**: Expert-thinking profile for Differential Geometer (theoretical / geometric analysis / gauge & index theory): Reasons from connections, curvature, and holonomy; fixes Lee vs Besse/MTW Riemann signs; uses SageManifolds/xAct/Cadabra, Chern–Weil and Atiyah–Singer index theory, and model-space checks (S^n, flat tori) while treating chart artifacts, torsion misuse, and CAS convention drift as first-class failure modes. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-geometer) · ★13)
- **differential-geometer**: Reasons from connections, curvature, and holonomy; fixes Lee vs Besse/MTW Riemann signs; uses SageManifolds/xAct/Cadabra, Chern–Weil and Atiyah–Singer index theory, and model-space checks (S^n, flat tori) while treating chart artifacts, torsion misuse, and CAS convention drift as first-class failure modes. - **differential-metabolite-abundance-analysis**: Use when joint spatial transcriptomics–metabolomics data have been integrated and clustered by SpatialMETA and you need cluster-defining genes/metabolites plus metabolite pathway enrichment for one sub-cluster vs. the rest. - **differential-metabolite-abundance-testing**: Use when a pixel x m/z feature matrix (e.g., 14,260 pixels x 3,044 ions from AFAD-ESI positive-mode mouse brain IMS at 100 um) has region assignments (auto-clusters or manual ROIs) and you need up/downregulated differential metabolites between two regions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-abundance-testing--8274b5843fb2ecb1))
- **differential-metabolite-abundance-testing**: Use when you have matrices normalized by different strategies (tGAM/rGAM/rLOESS, QC-RLSC, SERFF, TIGER) and need to quantify how the choice of normalization changes the differential abundance hit set. - **differential-metabolite-abundance-testing**: Use when you have preprocessed and normalized metabolite measurements from two or more distinct biological groups (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-annotation-counting))

- **differential-metabolite-detection-across-imputation-methods**: Decision support for whether missing-value imputation choices materially change the differential-metabolite lists and enriched pathways in an LC-MS untargeted metabolomics feature table, e.g. when a case study applied only one imputation method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-metabolite-detection-across-imputation-methods))

- **differential-methylation-region-testing**: Use when Bismark-aligned MethylC-seq (bisulfite) data in SAM/BAM format needs post-alignment processing to identify differentially methylated regions (DMRs) between two or more sample groups, e.g., IMR90 vs H1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-methylation-region-testing))
- **differential-methylation-results-filtering**: Use when you have a DMC results table from Illumina 450K/EPIC methylation analysis and need to restrict CpGs to promoter/5'UTR features or apply adjusted-P/logFC thresholds before visualization or enrichment. - **differential-network-edge-count-comparison**: Use when a two-condition metabolomics network has been estimated under alternative lambda/regularization settings and you must tabulate total vs case-specific vs control-specific vs shared edges to judge tuning impact. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-network-edge-count-comparison))
- **differential-network-inference-boosted-trees**: Use when you have two tab-separated gene expression matrices (same genes, rows=genes, columns=samples) from two biological conditions and need a condition-specific differential network for downstream pathway enrichment. - **differential-network-inference-from-transcriptomics**: Use when two tab-separated gene expression matrices (genes as rows, first column named 'Gene') from two conditions are available and the goal is a condition-specific differential regulatory network with enriched disease-relevant pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-network-inference-from-transcriptomics))
- **differential-network-module-agreement-thresholding**: Use when a DNEA object with estimated two-condition partial correlation networks (e.g. TEDDY metabolomics, 134 metabolites) must be partitioned into metabolic modules and you need to set or adjust the consensus agreement threshold (tau). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-network-module-agreement-thresholding))
- **differential-oracle-disclosure**: Response differentials disclose state. Use when method swaps change answers on one resource; when cached responses leak across sessions; when parallel channels disagree on the same object. - **differential-pathway-activation-analysis**: Use when scRNA-seq clusters and candidate LR pairs are available and the goal is to determine which intracellular signaling pathways are significantly activated per cluster and which LR interactions are functionally associated with them. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-activation-analysis))
- **differential-pathway-activity-prediction**: Use when you have spot-level (or cell-level) continuous feature values such as Seurat module scores plus 2D/3D spatial coordinates, and you need to rank gene sets or features by how non-randomly their activity is distributed across the tissue. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-activity-prediction))
- **differential-pathway-activity-t-testing**: Use when comparing two sample groups (e.g., DM2 vs NGT microarray profiles) to find pathways whose PLAGE-derived activity levels differ significantly, using t statistics calibrated by max-t label permutations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-pathway-activity-t-testing))
- **differential-pathway-activity-testing**: Use when single-cell transcriptomic data has been embedded in VEGA's pathway-informed latent space and you need to test which gene modules differ in activity between defined cell groups or conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-activity-testing))
- **differential-pathway-activity-testing-between-condition-groups**: Use when a normalized RNA-seq expression matrix with two condition groups (e.g., CF vs non-CF, or treated vs control) needs a benchmarked differential pathway activity comparison across rROMA and sample-wise scoring methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-activity-testing-between-condition-groups))
- **differential-pathway-ranking-comparison**: Use when comparing the computational efficiency (runtime, memory) and sensitivity of pathway activity scoring / differential pathway methods (scPAFA vs SCPA) on million-cell-scale scRNA-seq data with &gt;1000 pathways, or when assessing whether downsampling-based tools lose disease-relevant pathway. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/differential-pathway-ranking-comparison))
- **differential-peak-abundance-detection**: Use when after raw mass spectrometry data has been converted to a peak feature table (CSV or tabular format) containing mass-to-charge ratios, retention times, and intensity values across samples, and you need to reduce noise and identify which peaks show meaningful differential patterns between. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/differential-peak-abundance-detection) · 2 copies)
- **differential-peak-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a fitted MSI segmentation exists (e.g., spatial shrunken centroids in Cardinal on imzML-derived pig fetus liver/heart data) and you must report which peaks each segment differentially enriches or lacks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/differential-peak-enrichment-analysis))
- **differential-peak-motif-verification**: Use when two condition-specific differentially bound peak sets (e.g. female vs male GBM CC peaks) exist and the goal is to identify which TF motif families are enriched in one condition but not the other, reproducing published family-level enrichment patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/differential-peak-motif-verification))
- **differential-pr-significance-testing**: Use when per-bin m6A scores from Nanopore dRNA-seq tools are already computed and you need to quantify whether restricting the precision-recall analysis to motif-containing, exclusion-zone-free, or high-coverage bins significantly improves tool performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/differential-pr-significance-testing))
- **differential-privacy**: Use when an analytic release or machine-learning pipeline needs a formal differential-privacy guarantee against inference about individual records; define adjacency, budget, mechanism, composition, utility, and the exact release claim. ([sandbaseai/workbuddy-skill](https://github.com/sandbaseai/workbuddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-privacy) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **differential-privacy**: Use when you need to publish statistics or train models on sensitive data without exposing individuals. Covers epsilon budgets, Laplace and Gaussian noise, composition, and the practical limits of the guarantee. Also use when a data sharing request needs a principled privacy answer instead of "we anonymized it". ([mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal](https://github.com/mizazhaider-ceh/AgentArsenal/tree/HEAD/skills/privacy/differential-privacy))
- **differential-privacy**: Inject calibrated Laplace and Gaussian noise into analytics queries to guarantee $(\epsilon, \delta)$-differential privacy bounds. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/privacy/differential-privacy))
- **differential-privacy-basics**: Differential privacy (DP) basics for analytics and data releases: epsilon/delta privacy budgets, Laplace/Gaussian mechanisms, composition, privacy units, local vs central DP, and safe reporting of aggregates on owned datasets. Use when differential privacy, epsilon budget, DP-SGD awareness, private aggregates, noise addition for releases, or privacy engineering for internal metrics — not a substitute for legal counsel or de-identification certifications alone. ([bedkillerspacex-boop/codex-skill-library](https://github.com/bedkillerspacex-boop/codex-skill-library/tree/HEAD/differential-privacy-basics) · ★1)
- **differential-privacy-best-practices**: Use when applying differential privacy best practices. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-privacy-best-practices) · ★2)
- **differential-privacy-error-privacy-bud-9b26170890d64232**: Fixes the differential privacy failure privacy budget exhausted by applying the specific fix in the steps, proving it on one small scope first, then rerunning and reconciling counts. Use when this error blocks generation, validation or export. Not for platform outages, which must be resolved first. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-privacy-error-privacy-bud-9b26170890d64232))
- **differential-privacy-fundamentals**: Use when applying differential privacy fundamentals. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-privacy-fundamentals) · ★2)
- **differential-privacy-implementation**: Use when applying differential privacy implementation. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-privacy-implementation) · ★2)
- **differential-privacy-mechanisms**: Implement rigorous epsilon-delta differential privacy mechanisms (Laplace mechanism, Gaussian mechanism, Exponential mechanism) and DP-SGD (Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent) using Opacus and PyTorch. Track cumulative privacy loss budgets (Rényi Differential Privacy), clip gradient norms, and prevent membership inference attacks. Trigger when training ML models on sensitive data or releasing aggregated statistical datasets. ([hamzabellouch/agent-skills](https://github.com/hamzabellouch/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/Math/Differential_Privacy_and_Synthetic_Data/differential-privacy-mechanisms) · ★8)
- **differential-privacy-prod**: Deploy differential privacy in production systems including epsilon selection strategies, noise calibration with Laplace and Gaussian mechanisms, privacy budget tracking, composition theorems, and Python implementation patterns. For security history and blast radius; general review is /code-review-task. ([victorgmrqs/chat-with-your-docs](https://github.com/victorgmrqs/chat-with-your-docs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/differential-review))
- **differential-review**: Review security implications of code changes against a baseline. Use for security reviews of PRs, commits, uncommitted diffs, or removed validation; not for routine formatting or a claim of full-codebase audit coverage. Overlaps with harness-review and pr-review on code review; focuses on reachable security regressions. ([wzlwit/skillvault](https://github.com/wzlwit/skillvault/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/differential-review) · 2 copies)
- **differential-review-skill**: Audits GitHub Actions workflows for security vulnerabilities in AI agent integrations including Claude Code Action, Gemini CLI, OpenAI Codex, and GitHub AI Inference. Detects attack vectors where attacker-controlled input reaches AI agents running in CI/CD pipelines, including env var intermediary patterns, direct expression injection, dangerous sandbox configurations, and wildcard user allowlists. Use when reviewing workflow files that invoke AI coding agents, auditing CI/CD pipeline security for prompt injection risks, or evaluating agentic action configurations. ([Overdrive-Consulting/vcskills](https://github.com/Overdrive-Consulting/vcskills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-review-skill) · ★8)
- **differential-review-v2**: Differential Security Review workflow skill. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skills](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-review-v2) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **differential-rna-editing-analysis**: Use when you have per-sample RNA editing event tables (e.g. *_Edit_*_DATA.tsv from Mapit callediting) for an RBP treatment and matched control, and need to identify which transcripts are significant RBP binding targets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/differential-rna-editing-analysis))
- **differential-robot-and-sensors**: Assembling a differential drive robot in CoppeliaSim and getting it to actually move — joint axis and control mode, the friction Bullet obeys, mass and inertia, collision groups — plus proximity sensors, their blind zone, and why a wide cone sees the floor. ([KevinInoCol/coppeliasim-mcp](https://github.com/KevinInoCol/coppeliasim-mcp/tree/HEAD/skills/differential-robot-and-sensors) · ★1)

- **differential-scoring**: 差分判分定位：当总分高而未满（≥90% 但不到 100）、且本地各分量都'对'却不知哪块丢分时，用差分实验定位丢分分量/字段，再定点清除。核心：先定位后修复，禁止盲试。触发词：'差分判分'、'哪个分量掉分'、'定位丢分'、'高分未满'、'4% 缺口'、'差一点满分'。 ([Hab1nA/ASCodex](https://github.com/Hab1nA/ASCodex/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/differential-scoring) · 2 copies)
- **differential-security-review**: Use to review release, branch, PR, commit, or patch differences for changed trust boundaries, authorization, validation, parsing, networking, serialization, secrets, dependencies, CI, and blast radius. - **differential-security-review**: Analyze code diffs and PRs specifically for security implications, identifying newly introduced vulnerabilities and security regressions. - **differential-splicing**: Detects differential alternative splicing between conditions using rMATS-turbo (binomial LRT on junction counts), leafcutter (Dirichlet-multinomial GLM on intron clusters), MAJIQ V3 deltapsi/HET (Bayesian posterior on LSVs), SUPPA2 (empirical-null on TPM-derived PSI), or Shiba (junction-imbalance-corrected, 2025 SOTA at low coverage). - **differential-splicing-statistical-testing**: Use when you have LRTS (e.g. PacBio) bam files for two or more sample groups and need to detect differential splicing events (exon skipping, 5'/3' alternative splice sites, intron retention, mutually exclusive exons) or differential TSS/PAS usage at a stated FDR.
